A symmetry-based design further reduces confounding factors, allowing preferences across representation types to be compared systematically.
  We evaluate the framework on an executable mathematical benchmark with 550 conflict instances spanning 11 function families, comparing four representation types: pure natural language, formal language, naturalized formal language, and input--output examples. Results show systematic preference patterns rather than random behavior, with a consistent ordering: $ \text{Formal} \approx \text{Naturalized Formal} > \text{Pure Natural Language} > \text{Input--Output Examples} $. Example effects further depend on model capability and function family.
  We extend the framework to heterogeneous specification conflicts in Boolean algebra, code generation, and the clinical domain, demonstrating its applicability across diverse tasks and specification forms. The framework provides a unified approach for measuring how LLMs resolve conflicts between competing sources of information.

</details>


### [172] [QuantWAMs: Calibrating at the Right Granularity for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28405)
*Jiacheng Zhou,Jinfan Lv,Ruixuan Li,Longtai Zhang,Yan Wang,Wenqiang Zhang,Lizhe Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QuantWAMs是一个面向World Action Models (WAMs)的后训练量化(PTQ)框架，通过共享基异常值校准、共训练目标显著性分析和固定干预回滚审计三种策略，将量化决策与模型结构、回滚分布和任务目标对齐。在Fast-WAM和LingBot-VA上，W4A4设置下仿真结果与FP16仅差0.2-0.7个百分点，峰值内存降至FP16的29%，并提供1.4-1.6倍块级加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAMs联合预测未来观测和动作，但其迭代去噪和闭环执行导致部署成本高昂。现有PTQ方法因依赖开环目标、同质模型假设以及与部署情境不符的校准分布，不适合WAMs。因此需要一个考虑模型结构、回滚分布和任务目标的校准上下文对齐的量化框架。

Method: QuantWAMs提出三种策略：(1) 共享基异常值校准——仅跨坐标兼容模块池化激活证据；(2) 共训练目标显著性——基于视频-动作联合梯度计算经验Fisher分数，在校准稳定的层粒度上分配权重精度；(3) 固定干预回滚审计——在不改变精度预算的情况下，利用可达闭环状态修订去噪步骤保护调度。

Result: 在Fast-WAM和LingBot-VA上跨RoboTwin 2.0、LIBERO和AgiBot G2真实机器人操作任务评估。W4A4主导设置下仿真均值与FP16相差0.2-0.7个百分点。真实机器人试验验证了三种操作任务的部署可行性。视频和动作块的峰值权重-激活内存降至FP16的约29%，块级加速1.4-1.6倍。

Conclusion: QuantWAMs通过将量化决策与WAMs的部署上下文对齐，有效解决了现有PTQ方法在WAMs上的适用性问题，在保持任务性能的同时实现了显著的内存节省和推理加速，并在真实机器人上验证了部署可行性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) jointly predict future observations and actions, but their iterative denoising and closed-loop execution make efficient deployment costly. Existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods are poorly suited to WAMs because they rely on open-loop objectives, homogeneous model assumptions, and calibration distributions that do not reflect deployment. We present QuantWAMs, a PTQ framework that aligns quantization decisions with the calibration context defined by model structure, rollout distribution, and task objective. QuantWAMs introduces three strategies: shared-basis outlier calibration, which pools activation evidence only across coordinate-compatible modules; co-training-objective saliency, which computes empirical-Fisher scores from the joint video--action gradient and assigns weight precision at a calibration-stable layer granularity; and fixed-intervention rollout auditing, which revises denoising-step protection schedules using reachable closed-loop states without changing the precision budget. We evaluate QuantWAMs on Fast-WAM and LingBot-VA across RoboTwin 2.0, LIBERO, and real-robot manipulation with an AgiBot G2. Under a W4A4-dominant setting, the reported simulation means differ from FP16 by 0.2--0.7 percentage points. Real-robot trials further establish deployment feasibility on three manipulation tasks. For the targeted video and action blocks, QuantWAMs reduces peak weight-and-activation memory to about 29\% of FP16 and provides 1.4--1.6$\times$ block-level speedups.

</details>


### [173] [When Derived Measurements Mislead: Quantifying and Mitigating LLM Over-Trust with Privileged-Modality Reliability Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28421)
*Zongheng Guo,Tao Chen,Tianli Li,Mingzhe Cui,Yang Jiao,Lei Xie,Yi Pan,Xiao Hu,Manuela Ferrario*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文定义了派生特征过度信任（DFOT）问题——大语言模型将具有实例依赖有效性的派生测量当直接事实使用。以生理传感为案例，通过五个估计算量（COTR、CIR、CRR、ESRM、UHR）评估了LLM在冲突与误导情境下的信任误差、修复能力与效用损害，并在50,000对PPG-ECG记录上验证了框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 派生测量（如PPG推导的心律）越来越多地以直接事实形式进入LLM管道，但其有效性依赖具体实例。当前缺乏统一框架来量化和评估LLM在这种派生特征上的过度信任问题，需要一种可推广的评估目标和基准。

Method: 定义DFOT框架：以ECG为监督与离线参考（不展示给LLM），通过D1（派生心律与离线ECG冲突时是否过度信任）和D2（可靠派生心律在误导性严重病史下是否被错误拒绝）两个测试，配合五个估计算量（COTR、CIR、CRR、ESRM、UHR）量化信任、修复和效用损害。采用ECG-to-PPG特权蒸馏作为基线方法，在50,000对PPG-ECG记录上演示，并在187患者协议锁定测试集上评估。

Result: 在187患者测试集上，基线方法在四个修复与特异性指标上提升1.82-6.69个百分点，所有配对置信区间排除零；UHR增加0.67个百分点（95% CI: -0.4到+1.7）。框架不依赖特定可靠性生成器，具备通用性。

Conclusion: DFOT提供了一个统一的评估目标，使更强缓解方法能够在共同基准下被公平比较，推动解决派生测量误导LLM的系统性问题。

Abstract: Derived measurements increasingly enter large language model (LLM) pipelines as direct facts despite their instance-dependent validity. We define derived-feature over-trust (DFOT) as the failure in which a downstream LLM assigns such a measurement the epistemic status of a direct fact or uses it outside its valid scope. Using physiological sensing as a case study, D1 tests acceptance of a PPG-derived rhythm contradicted by offline ECG, whereas D2 tests rejection of an offline-confirmed reliable PPG rhythm under misleading severe history. ECG supplies training supervision and offline reference construction but is never shown to the LLM. Five estimands quantify this chain: conflict over-trust rate (COTR) and context-induced error rate (CIR) characterize D1/D2; correct repair rate (CRR) measures frozen-error repair; evidence-specific repair margin (ESRM) contrasts matched and patient-disjoint shuffled evidence; and utility harm rate (UHR) measures unnecessary verification among HIGH-reliability cases used without verification at baseline. The framework does not depend on a particular reliability generator. We demonstrate it on 50,000 paired PPG-ECG records using ECG-to-PPG privileged distillation as an illustrative baseline and PPG-only inference. On a protocol-locked 187-patient test, the baseline improves four repair and specificity endpoints by 1.82-6.69 percentage points, with all paired confidence intervals excluding zero; UHR increases by 0.67 percentage points (95% CI: -0.4 to +1.7). DFOT provides a common evaluation target for stronger mitigation methods. The code is available at https://github.com/Zongheng-Guo/When-Derived-Measurements-Mislead.

</details>


### [174] [A foundation model of numerical intelligence with cross-disciplinary generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28432)
*Chenghan Wu,Zongmin Yu,Liu Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了UNICON（UNified In-Context Operator Networks），一个具备"数值智能"（numerical intelligence）的基础模型。该模型能从数值上下文（而非语言）中推断和迁移任务相关的知识，跨学科（包括训练中未见过的学科）接近专家性能，且无需重新训练。与语言模型智能体结合后，在未见过的学科上甚至能超越最先进的专家模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类智能不仅体现在语言处理上，还体现在从数值数据中获取和应用知识的能力上。现有的基础模型（如大语言模型）主要聚焦于文本理解和生成，但科学与社会系统中存在大量需要从数值上下文中推断规律并应用于新任务的需求。作者希望填补这一空白，构建一个能够从数值上下文中进行上下文学习的基础模型。

Method: UNICON采用基于图结构示例作为上下文的机制：以来自同一系统的图示例作为上下文，推断这些示例间共享的预测关系，并将其应用于同一系统的查询中。在训练时通过多样化不同学科的数据来提升泛化能力。此外，UNICON可与语言模型智能体协同工作，以进一步提升性能。

Result: UNICON在多个科学与社会系统中（包括训练中未出现的学科）达到接近专家水平的性能，无需重新训练。与语言模型智能体结合后，在训练中未见过的学科中超越了最先进的专家模型。训练语料库的多样性显著提升了模型对未见学科的泛化能力。

Conclusion: UNICON确立为"数值智能"领域的基础模型，证明数值智能可以像语言智能一样通过上下文学习实现。它可作为更广泛人工智能生态系统的构建模块，促进跨学科的数值推理能力。

Abstract: Intelligence is commonly understood as the ability to acquire and apply knowledge, adapt to unfamiliar situations and solve new problems. Large language models exhibit this capacity by inferring task-relevant knowledge from textual context and applying it to new tasks. Yet intelligence need not be confined to language. For scientific and social systems, we need models that acquire and apply knowledge from numerical context-an ability we call numerical intelligence. Here we introduce UNified In-Context Operator Networks (UNICON), a foundation model that exhibits numerical intelligence across disciplines. Using graph-based examples from a system as context, UNICON infers the predictive relation shared across them and applies it to queries from the same system. Across scientific and social systems, including those from disciplines absent from training, the same model approaches specialist performance without retraining. Combining UNICON with language-model agents yields further gains, enabling it to surpass state-of-the-art specialists in a discipline unseen in training. We further show that training-corpus diversity improves generalization to unseen disciplines. Together, these results establish UNICON as a foundation model of numerical intelligence and position it as a building block for a broader ecosystem of artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [175] [LeanCSP: A Framework for Certifying Constraint Reformulation and Solving in Lean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28459)
*Pablo Manrique,Stefan Szeider*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在Lean定理证明器中引入了一个框架，同时解决了约束编程的两个验证层面：通过参数化证明整个问题族的语义保持性质（如等价性、可满足性等价和对称性破坏约束的正确性），以及通过翻译后端到MiniZinc、SMT-LIB和OPB等外部格式来检查求解器生成的证书。实验表明该框架在验证的对称性破坏方面可将求解器搜索工作量减少多达2×10^7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束编程是求解调度、规划、配置和验证等复杂组合问题的核心技术，但其结果的可信性需要两个层面的保证：前置重写/改革是否保持语义，以及求解器是否产生正确答案。现有方法缺乏同时覆盖这两层面的验证框架。

Method: 在Lean定理证明器中构建统一框架，支持：(1) 参数化证明问题族的公式级属性，包括等价性、可满足性等价和对称性破坏约束的正确性；(2) 通过翻译后端将约束问题转录为MiniZinc、SMT-LIB和OPB格式，检查外部求解器产生的证书。两个层面结合形成端到端工作流，无需信任外部求解器即可建立约束问题的可满足性或不可满足性。

Result: 验证的对称性破坏在实际中显著减少了求解器搜索工作量，每个问题族单个参数化证明被所有实例规模复用，搜索工作量减少因子高达2×10^7；整个Lean内验证过程开销可控，最大实例的验证耗时最多几分钟。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了约束编程求解结果的可信端到端验证，在不信任外部求解器的前提下建立问题满足性的确定性结论，同时兼顾了实际求解效率和验证开销的可行性。

Abstract: Constraint programming is a core technology for solving complex combinatorial problems in scheduling, planning, configuration, and verification. Trusting its results therefore demands guarantees at two levels: that reformulations applied beforehand are semantics-preserving, and that solvers produce correct answers. In this work, we introduce a framework that addresses both verification levels in the Lean theorem prover: it can be used to prove formulation-level properties, such as equivalence, equisatisfiability, and the correctness of symmetry-breaking constraints, parametrically for entire problem families; and to check solver-produced certificates for individual instances via translation backends to external formats such as MiniZinc, SMT-LIB, and OPB. Combining both levels yields an end-to-end workflow that establishes the satisfiability or unsatisfiability of a constraint problem without trusting the external solver. Experimental results show that our framework's verified symmetry breaking also pays off in practice: a single parametric proof per problem family, reused across all instance sizes, reduces solver search effort by a factor of up to 2x10^7, while the entire in-Lean certification stays affordable, taking at most a few minutes for our largest instances.

</details>


### [176] [A report-grounded vision-language foundation model for colonoscopy from 280000 routine reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28466)
*Jia Yu,Yan Zhu,Yili He,Zilong Wang,Xinyang Jiang,Peiyao Fu,Ruijie Yang,Tianyi Chen,Siyuan Li,Zhihua Wang,Fei Wu,Quanlin Li,Xian Yang,Pinghong Zhou,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了EndoCLIP，一个基于结肠镜报告的视觉-语言基础模型，通过从28万份常规结肠镜记录中逐步恢复出12.5万对病灶级图像-文本对进行训练。在病灶级图像-文本检索、结构化报告生成和六个多中心临床分类任务中均优于通用和生物医学视觉-语言编码器，其良恶性分类的线性探针在盲法研究中接近12名内镜专家的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结肠镜中的视觉-语言模型应用不足，尽管常规报告中包含丰富的专家描述（病灶外观、大小、位置），但这些报告是对整个操作过程的总结而非对单帧图像的标注，导致临床发现与对应图像之间缺乏强关联，限制了可扩展监督信号的利用。

Method: 从280,476份常规结肠镜记录中通过渐进式方法恢复出125,756个病灶级图像-文本对，并基于此训练EndoCLIP这一结肠镜专用视觉-语言基础模型。

Result: 在病灶级图像-文本检索、结构化报告生成和六个多中心临床分类任务中，EndoCLIP在零样本和线性探针设置下均优于通用及生物医学视觉-语言编码器；良恶性分类的线性探针性能接近12名内镜专家在盲法研究中的表现。

Conclusion: 恢复发现与帧之间的对应关系可以将常规文档转化为可扩展的监督信号，使得临床目标能够以语言形式指定，而无需针对每个任务进行单独标注。

Abstract: Vision-language models remain underused in colonoscopy despite the rich expert descriptions recorded in routine reports. These reports document lesion appearance, size and location but summarise entire procedures rather than caption individual frames, leaving clinical findings only weakly linked to the corresponding images. Here we develop EndoCLIP, a colonoscopy vision-language foundation model trained on 125,756 lesion-level image-text pairs progressively recovered from 280,476 routine colonoscopy records. Across lesion-level image-text retrieval, structured report generation and six multi-centre clinical classification tasks, EndoCLIP outperforms general-purpose and biomedical vision-language encoders in both zero-shot and linear-probe settings. On benign-versus-malignant classification, its linear probe approaches the performance of expert readers in a blinded study involving 12 endoscopists. These results suggest that recovering finding-to-frame correspondence can transform routine documentation into scalable supervision, enabling clinical targets to be specified in language rather than separately annotated for each task.

</details>


### [177] [Towards Autonomous Aircraft Surveillance from Nanosatellites through On-Board Inference and Generative Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28470)
*Antonio Delgado-Rosa,David Muñoz-Valero,Enrique Adrian Villarrubia-Martin,Juan Moreno-Garcia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种结合星上推理与生成式数据增强的工作流，通过在6U立方星上运行量化目标检测器，并利用扩散模型（经低秩适配微调）生成合成少数类航空器图像来平衡训练数据。结果显示全局mAP从77.9%提升至82.2%，少数类F1从0.683提升至0.811，检测器可在轨实现25-30帧/秒处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低轨航空监视面临两个相互关联的瓶颈：纳米卫星下行链路预算有限，但传统架构仍将海量原始图像传输至地面处理；同时公开的航空器卫星数据集稀缺且类别严重不平衡，导致标准检测器难以学习到稀有航空器类别的鲁棒表征。这两点限制要么延误决策时效，要么阻碍检测性能。

Method: 提出的工作流包含两大核心组件：一是在搭载低功耗边缘张量加速器的6U立方星上执行星上推理；二是通过低秩适配（LoRA）微调的扩散模型生成合成少数类图像，由中间检测器自动标注（伪标注），并与经典数据增强样本合并构建平衡数据集。

Result: 平衡数据集使全局平均精度（mAP）从77.9%提升至82.2%，少数类F1分数从0.683提升至0.811；量化检测器适配星载片上内存，在轨处理速度达25-30帧每秒。

Conclusion: 该工作流与传统的弯管式（bent-pipe）架构——卫星仅作为被动数据收集器的做法形成鲜明对比。计算实验支持该方案可作为纳米卫星实时自主航空监视的决策支持工具。

Abstract: Airborne surveillance from low Earth orbit is hindered by two interconnected bottlenecks: nanosatellites have a limited downlink budget, yet the conventional approach still transmits terabytes of raw imagery to the ground for processing, and open satellite datasets for aircraft are scarce and severely class-imbalanced. These limitations either delay timely decision-making or prevent standard detectors from learning robust representations of rare aircraft classes. In this paper, a workflow that combines on-board inference with generative data augmentation is proposed to address both limitations jointly. Inference is executed on a 6U CubeSat equipped with a low-power edge tensor accelerator, while a diffusion model fine-tuned through low-rank adaptation generates synthetic minority-class imagery. This synthetic output is automatically annotated, pseudo-labelled, by an intermediate detector and merged with classically augmented samples. The results show that the balanced dataset increases global mean average precision from 77.9% to 82.2%, with the minority class rising from F1=0.683 to F1=0.811, and that the quantised detector fits the on-chip memory and projects 25-30 frames per second on orbit. This approach contrasts with the conventional bent-pipe architecture, in which the satellite acts as a passive data collector. Therefore, the computational tests support the proposed workflow as a decision-support tool for real-time, autonomous airborne surveillance from nanosatellites.

</details>


### [178] [A Fuzzy Rule-based Neuro-Symbolic Approach for Pipe Severity Prediction in Sewer Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28481)
*Ngoc Thai Le,Thanh Ma,Umberto Straccia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模块化、基于模糊规则的神经符号框架，用于自动下水道管道严重程度评估。该框架将Swin Transformer的神经感知模块（预测14个多标签检查CODE度）与基于J48决策树转换为19条固定IF-THEN规则的符号推理模块解耦，并通过模糊逻辑（t-norm/s-norm算子）进行推断。在3,244张图像、五个高度不均衡严重度类别的数据集上，相比纯图像分类，准确率、平衡准确率、Macro F1和MCC分别提升17.9%、12.2%、23.0%和17.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动下水道管道严重程度评估依赖直接的图像分类，形成"黑箱"，视觉缺陷与最终严重度评分之间的关联隐含且不可追溯。作者旨在通过解耦神经感知与符号推理，构建一个可解释、可追溯的评估框架。

Method: 框架包含两个模块：(1) 感知模块使用Swin Transformer从图像直接预测14个多标签检查CODE度；(2) 推理模块使用Weka的J48决策树算法在真实CODE和严重度标签上训练，将决策树路径转换为19条固定IF-THEN规则。推断采用模糊逻辑：CODE条件的t-norm激活由规则置信度加权，再用对应s-norm组合产生可解释的类别证据。评估了Product、Łukasiewicz和Hamacher算子对。真实标签通过五个独立大语言模型对原始检查员备注的共识分析生成。

Result: 在3,244张图像、五个高度不均衡严重度类别的数据集上，相比纯图像分类，准确率、平衡准确率、Macro F1和MCC分别提升17.9%、12.2%、23.0%和17.3%。框架在保持竞争性类别均衡性能的同时，实现了从预测CODE度到规则支持和严重度证据的可追溯推理。

Conclusion: 该神经符号模糊推理框架在类不均衡数据集上取得了优于纯图像分类的性能，同时通过显式的模糊规则提供了可解释的推理链路，弥合了神经感知与符号推理之间的鸿沟。整体上实现了类别均衡性能与推理可追溯性的平衡。

Abstract: Standard automated sewer pipe severity assessment relies on direct image classification, creating a "black box" where the link between visual defects and final severity scores remains implicit. This study introduces a modular, fuzzy rule-based neuro-symbolic framework that bridges this gap by decoupling neural perception from symbolic reasoning. The perception module utilizes a Swin Transformer to predict 14 multilabel inspection CODE degrees directly from images. For reasoning, a DT, specifically Weka's J48, algorithm is trained on ground-truth CODEs and severity labels, and its paths are converted into 19 fixed IF--THEN rules. Inference operates via fuzzy logic: t-norm activations from CODE conditions are weighted by rule confidence and combined with corresponding s-norms to produce interpretable class evidence. We assessed Product, Łukasiewicz, and Hamacher operator pairs using a dataset of 3,244 images spanning five highly imbalanced severity classes. Ground-truth labels were robustly generated via consensus from five independent large language models analyzing original inspector notes. Our results show an improvement of accuracy, balanced accuracy, Macro F1 and MCC by 17.9%, 12.2%, 23.0%, and 17.3%, respectively, over image-only based classification.
  Overall, the framework combines competitive class-balanced performance with traceable reasoning from predicted CODE degrees to rule supports and severity evidence.

</details>


### [179] [SCOPE: Supply-Chain Operations through Coupled Policies for End-to-End Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28488)
*Yunhao Liang,Xianqi Cao,Pujun Zhang,Yuan Qu,Yongzhi Qi,Ningxuan Kang,Max Z. J. Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SCOPE（Supply-Chain Operations through Coupled Policies for End-to-End Coordination），一个将供应链实体建模为token并通过共享操作表征进行上下文关联的复合策略模型，用于统一的端到端补货规划。在叮咚买菜和京东的真实运营数据上验证，SCOPE在协调品类、供应商分配、补货频率和配送路由等多个耦合决策方面优于各阶段独立优化的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 供应链补货规划涉及品类选择、供应源分配、补货频率和配送路由等高度耦合的决策，但这些决策在现代供应链中通常由不同部门通过独立系统分别优化，导致缺货、库存积压和运输浪费。现有方法缺乏跨决策阶段、跨部门的端到端协调能力。

Method: SCOPE将供应链实体（如品类、仓库、供应商、配送点）表示为token，通过一个共享的运营表示层对其进行上下文编码，随后将每种token类型映射到对应的决策接口。每个决策在前序决策形成的部分计划基础上构建，完整计划则通过共享的系统级效用函数进行评估。框架在生鲜零售补货场景中实例化，涵盖服务频率、品类组合、产能压力和路网路由的强交互。

Result: 在叮咚买菜和京东两个不同补货层级的大规模供应链真实运营数据上，SCOPE一致优于分别优化各决策阶段的方法以及实践中常用的供应链运营基线与基准方法。

Conclusion: 学习并协调跨部门的运营耦合可以带来更有效的端到端供应链决策，表明供应链AI应当从孤立的决策模块走向统一的联合运营规划。

Abstract: Can supply-chain AI move beyond isolated decision modules toward unified operational planning? A complete replenishment plan specifies which products each location carries, which upstream facility supplies it, how often it is replenished, and how deliveries are routed. These decisions are operationally coupled: the selected assortment changes the demand and load passed to later stages; source assignment and replenishment frequency reshape the delivery requests; and route feasibility and cost, in turn, determine the system value of the earlier choices. Yet in modern supply chains, these decisions are often handled by separate departments and optimized through separate systems, which can lead to stockouts, inventory exposure, and avoidable transportation. We propose SCOPE: Supply-Chain Operations through Coupled Policies for End-to-End Coordination, a composite policy model that represents supply-chain entities as tokens, contextualizes them through a shared operational representation, and maps each token type to the corresponding decision interface. Each decision builds on the partial plan formed by earlier decisions while the completed plan is evaluated using a shared system-level utility. We instantiate this framework in urban fresh-retail replenishment, where service frequency, assortment, capacity pressure, and road-network routing interact strongly, and evaluate it on real operational data from Dingdong and JD.com, two large-scale supply chains operating at different replenishment echelons. Across both settings, SCOPE consistently outperforms methods that optimize each decision stage separately, as well as practice-oriented baselines commonly used in supply-chain operations. These results show that learning and coordinating cross-department operational couplings lead to more effective end-to-end supply-chain decisions.

</details>


### [180] [InfoOps Bench: A live information operations safety benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28503)
*Dorian Quelle,Lisa-Maria Neudert,Jonathan Bright,John Gallacher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个动态更新的AI基准测试（InfoOpsBench），用于评估前沿语言模型被用于国家级信息操作的完整性风险。基于实时监控2,100多个俄、中、伊国家支持的信息操作，对8家提供商的17个模型在四种提示框架下进行测试，发现绝大多数模型可被用于信息操作，完整性得分范围从8.8%到94.5%，模型大小无法解释这一巨大差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型可能被恶意行为者用于大规模信息操作，特别是被国家支持的信息资产所利用。现有静态基准容易饱和，且缺乏对模型在信息操作场景下完整性的系统评估，因此需要构建一个动态的、持续更新的基准来量化和管理这一风险。

Method: 从实时监控管道中收集超过2,100个俄、中、伊国家支持的信息操作，构建动态基准。对8家提供商的17个模型在四种提示框架下进行测试，计算完整性得分（拒绝请求的百分比）以及事实核查率等指标，并分析模型对国别关键声明的响应差异。

Result: 绝大多数模型可被用于信息操作；完整性得分范围从8.8%到94.5%，相差85.7个百分点且与模型规模无关。模型选择影响操作特征：有的模型会编造细节产生比源材料更有害的输出，有的在合规时消解声明。事实核查率从2.9%到72.9%不等。除Z.ai的GLM 5.2外，中国开发的模型在面对事实依据充分但批评中国的声明时，合规性急剧下降48-70个百分点。

Conclusion: 模型完整性对抗信息操作与模型对良性内容的拒答倾向部分相关，反映了在模型可用性与安全性之间平衡的挑战。模型在涉及本国关键利益议题时展现出显著的政治偏向性，这提示需要更加注重模型的完整性和中立性评估。

Abstract: In this paper we present an active, constantly updated AI benchmark which measures the integrity of frontier language models against being co-opted for state-backed information operations. We draw on over 2,100 information operations from a live monitoring pipeline which tracks Russian, Chinese and Iranian state-backed information assets. Alongside this paper, we release a companion website that tracks the most prominent claims spread by state-backed media outlets, updated weekly, available from: pattrn.ai/research/infoopsbench. The dynamic nature of the benchmark makes it resistant to saturation.
  In the benchmark, we test 17 models from 8 providers across four prompt framings. We find that most models can be co-opted for information operations. Integrity scores, defined as the percentage of refused requests, range from 8.8% to 94.5%, an 85.7-percentage-point spread not explained by model size. Model choice also changes the character of the resulting operation. Some models fabricate details and produce output more harmful than the source material, others defuse claims even while complying, and fact-checking rates vary from 2.9% to 72.9%.
  Integrity against information operations is at least partly related to refusal to produce content even for benign claims, illustrating the challenge of balancing model usability with safety. With one exception (Z.ai's GLM 5.2), the Chinese-developed models sharply cut compliance on factually grounded but China-critical claims, dropping 48-70 percentage points relative to matched benign claims.

</details>


### [181] [Selective Credibility-Limited Belief Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28523)
*Theofanis Aravanis,Costas D. Koutras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出选择性可信度受限信念更新（selective credibility-limited belief update），允许将复合认知输入按源世界转换为更弱的代理后再进行受限转变，统一了Katsuno-Mendelzon信念更新与可信度受限信念更新，并刻画了两类表现良好的子类算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可信度受限信念更新方法将认知输入视为不可分割的整体，无法表达复合输入仅部分可实现的情形。需要一种更通用的框架来处理源依赖的选择性接受。

Method: 提出选择性可信度受限信念更新框架：每个源世界先对认知输入施加变换函数生成较弱代理，再执行可信度受限转变。给出了对应的语义与公理刻画，并识别两类子类算子：一致性保持更新算子和最大一致性保持更新算子。

Result: 通过证明可信度受限信念更新为该框架的特殊情形，以及Katsuno-Mendelzon信念更新在去掉可信度限制且变换函数取恒等函数时涌现，确立了框架的通用性和更强的表达能力。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一个统一且更具表达力的信念更新理论，既涵盖已有方法，又支持源依赖的选择性接受，填补了复合认知输入部分实现的空白。

Abstract: Belief update concerns changes in an agent's beliefs induced by changes in the underlying world. Standard Katsuno-Mendelzon update assumes that an epistemic input can be incorporated from every initially possible world, whereas credibility-limited belief update restricts, for each source world, the successor worlds regarded as credible or reachable. Nevertheless, existing credibility-limited approaches treat the epistemic input as an indivisible whole, and therefore cannot represent cases in which only part of a compound epistemic input can be realized. We introduce selective credibility-limited belief update, in which the epistemic input is transformed, relative to each source world, into a weaker proxy before the credibility-limited transition is performed. We provide semantic and axiomatic characterizations of the resulting class of update operators. We then identify two well-behaved sub-classes; namely, consistency-preserving update operators, which require every transformed epistemic input to be credible from its source world whenever the original epistemic input is consistent, and maximal consistency-preserving update operators, which additionally require the selected proxy to be maximally informative among the credible consequences of the original epistemic input. Finally, we establish the generality of the proposed framework by showing that credibility-limited belief update is recovered as a special case, while Katsuno--Mendelzon belief update emerges when credibility restrictions are removed and the transformation functions are taken to be identities. These results demonstrate that the framework provides a unified and strictly more expressive account of belief update, encompassing established approaches while supporting source-dependent selective acceptance.

</details>


### [182] [MANTA: Multi-Agent Network Topology Adaptation for Self-Evolving Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28527)
*Mao-xun Huang,Jerry Wang,Yi-Cheng Lai,Zhengxin Zhang,Claire Cardie,Hen-Hsen Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MANTA是一个让多智能体系统的通信拓扑在推理时自我进化的框架。它在部署前基于先验经验初始化任务相关的拓扑，部署中监控协作轨迹并在必要时进行有界的结构更新（可修改角色、通信链接、执行顺序、信息可见性和验证路径），在五个基准上达到平均74.0分，比最强基线高5.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型多智能体系统通常将通信拓扑视为固定设计选择或离线优化目标，无法在推理时根据任务实际需求自适应调整协作结构，限制了系统的灵活性和性能上限。

Method: MANTA包含两个阶段：(1)执行前，基于先验结构经验初始化一个任务条件下的拓扑；(2)部署中，监控协作轨迹，当当前组织不再充分时应用有界的结构更新，可修改智能体角色、通信链接、执行顺序、信息可见性和验证路径，同时保持任务接口和智能体预算不变。

Result: 在五个基准（涵盖信息检索、工具使用、规划、工作流执行和数学推理）上，MANTA达到最高平均分74.0，比最强基线高出5.8个百分点，并在PlanCraft上取得最佳结果。

Conclusion: 推理时的自我改进可以扩展到协作本身的架构层面，证明多智能体系统的通信结构能够在部署过程中动态适应任务需求，而非局限于固定的或预优化的设计。

Abstract: Large language model-based multi-agent systems improve complex problem solving through task decomposition, agent specialization, information exchange, and intermediate validation. However, existing systems typically treat communication topology as a fixed design choice or an offline optimization target. We introduce MANTA, a framework for Multi-Agent Network Topology Adaptation that enables communication structures to self-evolve at inference time. Before execution, MANTA initializes a task-conditioned topology from prior structural experience. During deployment, it monitors collaboration traces and applies bounded structural updates when the current organization becomes insufficient. These updates can modify agent roles, communication links, execution order, information visibility, and validation pathways while preserving the task interface and agent budget. We evaluate MANTA against representative single-agent and multi-agent baselines on five benchmarks spanning information seeking, tool use, planning, workflow execution, and mathematical reasoning. MANTA achieves the highest average score of 74.0, outperforming the strongest baseline by 5.8 percentage points and obtaining the best result on PlanCraft. These results show that inference-time self-improvement can extend to the architecture of collaboration itself.

</details>


### [183] [Rethinking Inference-Time Scaling in Local Computer-Use Agents: Failure Modes and Compute Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28573)
*Woongkyu Lee,Jungwook Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了本地部署的计算机使用智能体（CUA）在严格硬件约束下推理时扩展（inference-time scaling）的效果，涵盖上下文、时间、结构和并行四个维度。实验表明额外计算往往产生边际收益递减且会改变失败模式，并提出高效本地CUA需要选择性计算分配、故障感知控制机制以及围绕本地模型能力设计的智能体框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地部署CUA对隐私、成本效率和实际可用性日益重要，但严格硬件约束下如何提升其性能仍是挑战。虽然推理时扩展已被证明能提升前沿CUA的性能，但其对资源受限的本地模型是否有效尚不清楚，需要系统性的实证研究来填补这一空白。

Method: 在Qwen3-VL-8B/30B-A3B、UI-TARS-1.5-7B和OpenCUA-7B等本地模型上，基于OSWorld基准，从情境（contextual）、时间（temporal）、结构（structural）和并行（parallel）四个维度系统评估推理时扩展策略的效果。

Result: 额外计算常产生递减收益且改变失败模式：情境扩展提供历史依据并改善轨迹稳定性与任务准确率，但收益会趋于饱和且失败由重复/停滞轨迹转向过早虚假成功；时间扩展减少最大步数停滞但无法显著提升任务成功率；结构分解在本地两阶段智能体中引入规划和格式开销；并行扩展可部分缓解这些失败但成本高昂。

Conclusion: 高效本地CUA需要选择性计算分配、故障感知控制机制以及围绕本地模型的能力与局限性设计的智能体框架，而非简单地增加推理计算量。

Abstract: Deploying autonomous computer-use agents (CUAs) locally is increasingly important for privacy, cost efficiency, and practical usability, yet improving their performance under strict hardware constraints remains challenging. While recent studies show that inference-time scaling can improve frontier computer-use agents through additional computation during execution, its effectiveness for resource-constrained local models remains poorly understood. We present a systematic empirical study of inference-time scaling in local CUAs across contextual, temporal, structural, and parallel dimensions. We evaluate Qwen3-VL-8B/30B-A3B, UI-TARS-1.5-7B, and OpenCUA-7B on the OSWorld benchmark. Our results show that additional computation often yields diminishing returns while changing failure modes. Contextual scaling provides historical grounding that improves trajectory stability and task accuracy, but its gains saturate as token cost increases and failures shift from repetitive or stalled trajectories toward premature false successes. Temporal scaling similarly reduces max-step stalls, yet does not substantially improve task success, indicating that longer horizons often extend erroneous trajectories rather than correct them. We further find that structural decomposition can introduce planning and formatting overhead in local two-stage agents, while parallel scaling partially mitigates these failures at a substantial computational cost. Overall, our findings suggest that efficient local CUAs require selective compute allocation, failure-aware control mechanisms, and agentic frameworks designed around the capabilities and limitations of local models.

</details>


### [184] [DualG-MRAG: Decoupling Macro-Reasoning and Micro-Matching for Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28580)
*Jiacheng Tao,Qingyun Sun,Haonan Yuan,Ziwei Zhang,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DualG-MRAG是一种双层级框架，通过解耦的宏观推理图和微观匹配图来解决多模态检索增强生成（MM-RAG）中复杂的多跳推理任务，兼顾全局结构推理与细粒度证据匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MM-RAG方法主要依赖独立的实例级匹配，难以捕捉跨模态和跨文档的显式关系；图增强方法虽引入结构建模，但在多模态场景下面临困境：细粒度视觉特征导致图快速膨胀和检索噪声，而粗粒度表示又会丢弃关键局部证据。

Method: 提出DualG-MRAG双层级框架，包含：(1) 解耦的Macro Graph（宏观图，用于全局拓扑路由）和Micro Graph（微观图，用于精确局部验证），以隔离全局结构推理与细粒度证据匹配来抑制检索噪声；(2) 将检索建模为查询驱动的消息传递过程，通过GNN检索器实现跨异构证据源的动态相关性传播；(3) 引入动态规划解码机制，直接从GNN前向传播中提取显式推理路径，替代标准的孤立文档块输入。

Result: 大量实验表明，DualG-MRAG在证据召回率和复杂问答准确率方面均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: DualG-MRAG通过双层级解耦架构有效解决了多模态RAG中全局结构推理与细粒度证据匹配的冲突，在复杂多跳推理任务上取得了优于现有方法的性能。

Abstract: While Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (MM-RAG) has shown promising results, it still struggles with complex multi-hop reasoning tasks. Existing methods primarily focus on independent instance-level matching, which often fails to capture explicit relationships across modalities and documents. Although Graph-enhanced methods introduce structural modeling, they face a fundamental challenge in multimodal scenarios: incorporating fine-grained visual features leads to rapid graph expansion and retrieval noise, whereas coarse-grained representations cause the discarding of critical local evidence. To address this dilemma, we propose DualG-MRAG, a Dual-tier framework that introduces a decoupled architecture comprising Macro-reasoning and Micro-matching Graphs for Multimodal RAG. Specifically, to suppress retrieval noise by isolating global structural reasoning from fine-grained evidence matching, we construct a Macro Graph for global topological routing and a Micro Graph for precise local verification. Subsequently, to enable dynamic relevance propagation across heterogeneous evidence sources, we formulate retrieval as a query-driven message passing process via a GNN Retriever. Furthermore, to provide the generative model with coherent structural guidance, we introduce a dynamic programming decoding mechanism that extracts explicit reasoning paths directly from the GNN's forward pass, replacing the standard input of isolated document chunks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DualG-MRAG outperforms baselines in both evidence recall and complex QA accuracy.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [Entropy-Smooth Convex Optimization Cannot Be Accelerated](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27476)
*Jacob M. Aguirre,Dmitrii M. Ostrovskii*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在关于负熵相对L-光滑的凸函数类上，一阶方法的最小化收敛率存在Ω(L/T)的下界（当d=Ω(T²)时），说明镜像下降法在该类中至多差一个对数因子即达到最优，并且无法加速。结果还推广到了量子设置（关于负von Neumann熵相对光滑的函数类）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Dragomir等人已证明在相对光滑假设下加速可能不可能，但其反例使用的prox函数是病态的、与困难实例一起构造的。本文旨在表明，即使对于结构良好的具体prox函数（如负熵），加速仍然不可能，从而填补了这一空白。

Method: 作者通过构造满足关于负熵相对L-光滑性质的凸函数类中的困难实例，对所有一阶方法建立统一的最坏情况收敛下界Ω(L/T)。证明中要求维度d=Ω(T²)。该构造方法还通过类比扩展到量子设置，在d×d埃尔米特正半定矩阵（迹为1）的谱面体上，针对关于负von Neumann熵相对光滑的函数类证明相同的下界。

Result: 在关于负熵相对L-光滑的凸函数类中，所有一阶方法的收敛率下界为Ω(L/T)（当d=Ω(T²)时）。这表明镜像下降在该类中是最优的（至多差一个对数因子），尽管在ℓ1-范数光滑假设下加速方法是可用的。该结果也推广到了量子设置，在关于负von Neumann熵相对光滑的函数类上得到相同的下界。

Conclusion: 本文证明确实存在结构良好的prox函数（负熵），在其对应的相对光滑凸函数类中加速是不可能的，一阶方法的收敛率下界为Ω(L/T)，镜像下降为最优算法（差一个对数因子）。这澄清了相对光滑框架下加速可行的边界条件，并一致性地将结论推广到了量子领域。

Abstract: We prove an $Ω(L/T)$ lower bound for the convergence rate of minimization in the class of functions that are convex and $L$-smooth relative to negative entropy on the standard $d$-simplex, valid for every first-order method when $d = Ω(T^2)$. In particular, this shows that mirror descent is optimal up to a logarithmic factor in this class. This may be surprising due to the fact that accelerated methods are readily available under the assumption of smoothness in $\ell_1$-norm. While Dragomir et al. (Mathematical Programming, 2022) have already showed that acceleration might be impossible under relative smoothness, their prox-function is pathological and constructed together with the hard instance. In contrast, we show non-acceleration for a specific prox-function with particularly favorable structure. We also extend the result to the quantum setting, proving the same lower bound in the class of functions $L$-smooth relative to negative von Neumann entropy on the spectrahedron of $d \times d$ Hermitian positive-semidefinite matrices with unit trace.

</details>


### [186] [Branching out: Prognostics-Based Replacement Policies for Series Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27899)
*Daniel Koutas,Daniel Straub*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合规划方法，用于推导基于预测的预测性维护策略，该方法结合决策选项、预后模型提供的系统未来状态信息以及更新-报酬过程的成本，生成只需少量参数即可定义的策略，并通过数值实验证明其在预防性更换和预防性订购两种场景中性能接近优化基准策略，且更高效、对过拟合更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测性维护策略的制定需要综合考虑决策选项、预后模型的信息以及维护成本，现有方法可能过于复杂或容易过拟合，缺乏一种既高效又鲁棒的混合规划方法来推导基于预后的维护策略。

Method: 提出一种混合规划方法，整合了三种关键要素：(1)可用的决策选项，(2)预后模型提供的系统未来状态信息，(3)底层更新-报酬过程的成本结构。该方法生成仅由少量参数定义的策略，这些参数可通过理论推导或基于运行-失效数据的优化来确定。

Result: 在两种预测性维护决策场景（预防性更换和预防性订购）中进行了数值实验，结果表明推导出的策略在性能上与优化的基准策略相当，同时显著更高效且对过拟合更鲁棒。

Conclusion: 所提出的混合规划方法能够以极简参数形式生成高效且鲁棒的预测性维护策略，在不牺牲性能的前提下大幅提升策略的可解释性和泛化能力。

Abstract: We propose a hybrid planning method for deriving prognostics-based predictive maintenance policies. The method accounts for the available decision options, the information on the future state of the system provided by a prognostic model, and the costs of the underlying renewal-reward process. It results in policies defined by only a few parameters, which can be determined based on theoretical considerations or by optimization from run-to-failure data. We demonstrate the potential of the method in two separate predictive maintenance decision settings: preventive replacement and preventive ordering. Numerical investigations show that the derived policies rival the performance of optimized benchmark policies, while being significantly more efficient and robust against overfitting.

</details>


### [187] [The Existence and Stability of Generalized Multi-Source Weber Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27900)
*V. S. T. Long,N. M. Nam,T. H. Tai,C. Tammer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在最小时间函数框架下研究具有集值目标的一般化多源Weber问题，建立了解的存在性、解集的定性性质（闭性、紧性、有界/无界条件）、目标函数与最优值函数关于目标集扰动的Lipschitz连续性，以及全局和局部解映射的稳定性分析，提供了该问题的定量与定性敏感性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Weber问题（选址问题）通常假设目标为单点集，但在实际应用中目标可能是不确定或集值的。本文旨在将多源Weber问题推广到集值目标的情形，并基于最小时间函数这一工具，系统研究其解的存在性、结构性质及参数扰动下的稳定性，从而拓展Weber问题的理论框架。

Method: 本文采用最小时间函数作为分析框架，主要方法包括：利用极值原理和变分分析工具建立全局与局部最优解的存在性；通过集合拓扑性质分析解集的闭性、紧性及有界/无界判别条件；运用Lipschitz分析和集值分析技术推导目标函数与最优值函数关于目标集扰动的Lipschitz连续性；引入全局与局部解映射，借助集值映射的稳定性理论（如上下半连续性）研究其灵敏度性质。

Result: 建立了多源Weber问题在最小时间函数框架下全局和局部最优解的存在性定理；刻画了解集的闭性、紧性以及有界/无界的充分条件；证明了目标函数和最优值函数关于目标集扰动的显式Lipschitz连续性；并获得了全局和局部解映射在集值分析意义下的稳定性结果。

Conclusion: 本文为一般化多源Weber问题提供了一套完整的定量与定性敏感性分析理论，将经典的Weber选址问题从点目标扩展到集值目标，并验证了最小时间函数框架在该类问题中的有效性和适用性。

Abstract: This paper studies the generalized multi-source Weber problem with set-valued targets in the framework of minimal time functions. We first establish the existence of global and local optimal solutions and investigate several qualitative properties of the corresponding solution sets, including closedness, compactness, and conditions ensuring boundedness or unboundedness. Next, we derive explicit Lipschitz continuity properties for the objective function and the associated optimal value function with respect to perturbations of the target sets. We then introduce the global and local solution mappings and study their stability properties from the viewpoint of set-valued analysis. These results provide a quantitative and qualitative sensitivity analysis for the generalized multi-source Weber problem in the setting of minimal time functions.

</details>


### [188] [OptGraph: Large Language Models Enhanced Evolutionary Optimization Via Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27918)
*Xianchao Xiu,Jianhao Li,Huangyue Chen,Wanquan Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: OptGraph是首个引入图检索增强生成（GraphRAG）的优化智能体工作流，通过构建可复用的类型化经验图并结合图邻域信息增强检索，实现跨多样任务的自动化进化优化，在基准数据集上平均精确准确率比最先进的基于提示的方法高出8.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的自动化进化优化方法存在三个主要局限：模式复用能力不足、缺乏错误感知的细化机制、跨任务检索鲁棒性差。

Method: OptGraph首先将可复用经验构建为类型化图，捕获建模模式、问题形式化、实现细节和错误修正之间的关系；推理阶段利用图的邻域信息丰富检索知识，提供结构化上下文以改进建模、验证和迭代细化；同时支持自适应知识更新，将执行轨迹和验证反馈蒸馏为可复用的图知识，无需进行LLM参数微调。

Result: 在基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，OptGraph相比最先进的基于提示的自动化优化框架，平均精确准确率提升了8.9%。

Conclusion: OptGraph通过图结构化的知识表示与检索增强机制，有效解决了LLM在进化优化中模式复用和错误细化的局限性，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as a powerful tool for automated evolutionary optimization, but existing methods remain limited in pattern reuse, error-aware refinement, and retrieval robustness across diverse tasks. To address these limitations, we propose OptGraph, the first optimization agentic workflow that introduces graph retrieval-augmented generation (GraphRAG). Specifically, OptGraph first constructs reusable experience as a typed graph, capturing the relationships among modeling patterns, problem formalization, implementation details, and error corrections. In the inference stage, OptGraph leverages graph neighborhood information to enrich retrieved knowledge, providing structured context to improve modeling, verification, and iterative refinement. Moreover, OptGraph supports adaptive knowledge updates, enabling the distillation of execution traces and verification feedback into reusable graph knowledge without ndertaking LLM parameter tuning. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets show that our proposed OptGraph achieves an average exact accuracy 8.9% higher than the state-of-the-art prompt-based automated optimization frameworks. Our code has been made available at https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/OptGraph.

</details>


### [189] [Global exponential turnpike properties for optimal control of the viscous Burgers equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28042)
*Emmanuel Trélat,Xingwu Zeng,Can Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对一维黏性Burgers方程在局部内部控制下的二次最优跟踪问题，建立了全局指数turnpike性质。对所有初值，有限时域最优解在周期跟踪目标足够小时趋近唯一的周期最优运行状态；零目标情形在原点处建立全局稳态turnpike且无需对初值做小性假设。这是黏性Burgers方程首个全局指数turnpike结果，证明结合严格凸性与周期Riccati理论的局部指数turnpike和抛物耗散论证（提供不依赖时域的耗散时间）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者希望填补黏性Burgers方程在全局指数turnpike性质方面的空白。此前相关工作仅涉及局部结果或线性系统，而针对非线性抛物型偏微分方程（特别是Burgers方程）的全局turnpike性质尚未被建立，因此本文旨在给出首个全局结果。

Method: 证明分为两部分：一是通过严格凸性和周期Riccati理论获得局部指数turnpike估计；二是利用抛物方程耗散特性构造一个与时间时域无关的耗散吸收时间（absorbing time），将任意初值演化到局部吸引域内，从而将局部结果延拓为全局结果。对于零目标情形，可直接在原点建立全局稳态turnpike。

Result: 建立了黏性Burgers方程二次最优跟踪问题在局部内部控制下的全局指数turnpike性质：对足够小的周期跟踪目标，所有初值对应的有限时域最优解指数逼近唯一周期最优运行；对零目标情形，建立了无需初值小性假设的全局稳态turnpike。

Conclusion: 本文首次证明黏性Burgers方程的全局指数turnpike性质，统一了局部turnpike理论和抛物耗散结构的论证思路，为该类非线性偏微分方程控制问题研究提供了新的理论工具，也为后续扩展到其他抛物型非线性系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: We establish global exponential turnpike properties for quadratic optimal tracking problems governed by the one-dimensional viscous Burgers equation with localized internal control. For every initial datum, finite-horizon optimal solutions approach the unique optimal periodic regime when the periodic tracking target is sufficiently small; the zero-target case yields a global steady turnpike at the origin, with no smallness assumption on the initial datum. To our knowledge, these are the first global exponential turnpike results for the viscous Burgers equation. The proof combines a local exponential turnpike, obtained through strict convexity and periodic Riccati theory, with a parabolic dissipation argument that provides an absorbing time independent of the horizon.

</details>


### [190] [Multi-Asset Utility Maximization with Jump Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28051)
*Sigui Brice Dro*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究多维布朗运动与独立齐次泊松随机测度驱动的风险资产组合效用最大化问题，允许策略包含跳跃信号。采用鞅最优性原理推导带跳的倒向随机微分方程（BSDE），证明其解的存在唯一性，并处理投资者接收异质跳跃信号的特定情形，最后提供高斯信号下对数效用的数值示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将一维指数效用情形下的研究方法扩展到多维布朗运动与跳跃过程驱动的更一般框架，解决策略中包含跳跃信号时的组合效用最大化问题，并考虑投资者接收异质跳跃信号的特殊情况。

Method: 首先将组合动态表示为半鞅过程；然后利用鞅最优性原理推导带跳的BSDE，以刻画值函数和最优策略；随后证明该带跳BSDE解的存在唯一性；并针对异质跳跃信号的特定情形进行分析；最后提供高斯信号下对数效用的数值模拟。

Result: 成功推导了多维情形下带跳的BSDE以表征值函数与最优策略，证明了该BSDE解的存在唯一性，并在异质跳跃信号情形下进行了理论分析和数值验证。

Conclusion: 通过BSDE方法，本文为多维布朗运动与跳跃过程驱动的组合效用最大化问题提供了完整的理论框架，涵盖同质与异质跳跃信号情形，数值结果验证了理论方法的有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study portfolio utility maximization problem in a setting where the risky asset is driven by a multidimensional Brownian motion and an independent homogeneous Poisson random measure, and where strategies may incorporate jump signals. Following the same approach as in the one-dimensional case for the exponential utility function [17], we first represent the portfolio dynamics as semimartingale processes. We then use martingale optimality principle to derive the corresponding backward stochastic differential equation (BSDE) with jumps to characterize both value function and an optimal strategy in terms of its solution. We subsequently prove the existence and uniqueness of the solution to the related BSDE with jumps. We also address the idiosyncratic case i.e where the investor receive heterogeneous jump signals. Finally, we provide numerical illustrations with Gaussian signals for the logarithmic case.

</details>


### [191] [On Leader Selection for Strong Structural Controllability in Matrix-Weighted Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28168)
*Lanhao Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套严格的数学框架，解决矩阵加权网络中最小领导者集合选择的强结构可控性（SSC）逆综合问题。通过证明结构不可控性仅源于维度特定的可达性隔离和拓扑对称等价性，设计了两阶段综合方法：可达性前置阶段识别结构根，以及三种对称破缺算法（贪心WL选择、子模边界最大化、划分熵最大化），并通过数学证明和数值实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵加权网络中的最小领导者集合选择以保证强结构可控性是一个尚未解决的NP-hard挑战，现有方法难以在多项式时间内有效求解，需要建立严格的数学框架来系统性地解决这一问题。

Method: 提出两阶段综合方法：(1) 可达性前置阶段，用于识别结构根，排除可达性隔离导致的不可控性；(2) 三种对称破缺算法——贪心Weisfeiler-Lehman选择、子模边界最大化、划分熵最大化——用于克服拓扑对称等价性带来的不可控瓶颈。同时通过数学证明保证对不变子空间和结构膨胀的免疫性。

Result: 通过数学证明证明了所提出方法对不变子空间和结构膨胀具有免疫性，并在多种拓扑结构上的广泛数值评估中验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为矩阵加权网络中的SSC逆综合问题提供了首个严格的数学框架，通过将NP-hard问题分解为可达性处理和对称破缺两个可处理的子问题，实现了对最小领导者集合的高效求解。

Abstract: The inverse synthesis problem of selecting a minimal leader set to guarantee strong structural controllability (SSC) in matrix-weighted networks remains an unresolved NP-hard challenge. This paper proposes a rigorous mathematical framework to solve this. We prove that structural uncontrollability stems exclusively from dimension-specific reachability isolation and topological symmetry equivalence. To overcome these bottlenecks, we formulate a two-phase synthesis: a reachability prerequisite to identify structural roots, followed by three distinct symmetry-breaking algorithms (Greedy Weisfeiler-Lehman Selection, Submodular Bound Maximization, and Partition Entropy Maximization). Mathematical proofs guarantee immunity to invariant subspaces and structural dilation, validated by extensive numerical evaluations across diverse topologies.

</details>


### [192] [The optimality of an (s, S) hiring policy on a workforce planning problem with fixed recruitment costs and binomial turnover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28171)
*Zhen Chen,Roberto Rossi,Belen Martin-Barragan,S. Armagan Tarim*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限时域劳动力规划问题，其中员工流失服从二项分布且参数依赖于招聘后的员工水平，并包含固定招聘成本。通过引入Binomial-K-凸性概念，证明了最优招聘策略具有(s,S)型结构，并提出了混合整数线性规划(MILP)的分段逼近解法，数值实验显示该方法计算快速且最优性间隙小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的劳动力规划面临员工流失率的随机性和依赖决策的转移概率，而传统随机动态规划方法难以处理此类决策依赖的转移概率和固定招聘成本并存的问题，需要从理论上建立最优策略的结构性质并提供高效求解算法。

Method: 研究建立有限时域随机动态规划模型，将每期员工流失建模为参数依赖于招聘后员工水平的二项分布，并纳入固定招聘成本。理论方面，引入Binomial-K-凸性概念，证明变量单期成本函数的离散凸性以及期望总成本在二项传播下保持K-凸性，从而推导出最优策略的(s,S)型结构。算法方面，提出分段逼近方法将问题转化为MILP，用于求解各周期的(s,S)参数。

Result: 证明了在二项式传播下Bellman函数保持K-凸性，从而确定最优招聘策略具有(s,S)型结构：当员工水平低于阈值s时招至S，否则不招聘。提出的分段逼近MILP方法能够有效求解问题并计算各期的(s,S)参数，数值实验表明该方法计算速度较快且最优性间隙较小。

Conclusion: 该研究为具有决策依赖转移概率和固定成本的劳动力规划问题提供了坚实的理论分析框架和高效的数值求解方法。Binomial-K-凸性概念的引入推广了经典K-凸性理论，为处理二项式随机传播下的库存/招聘决策提供了新工具，同时MILP方法保障了实际应用的可行性与计算效率。

Abstract: We study a finite-horizon workforce planning problem in which staff turnover in each period follows a binomial distribution whose parameters depend on the post-hiring workforce level. The model incorporates a fixed hiring cost that is incurred whenever recruitment occurs, regardless of the number of employees hired. The objective is to minimise the expected total cost, including recruitment, salary, and shortage costs, where deviations below period-specific staffing requirements are penalised. To analyse this stochastic dynamic programme with decision-dependent transition probabilities, we establish the discrete convexity of the variable single-period cost (the sum of expected salary and penalty costs) and the K-convexity of the expected total cost. Specifically, we introduce the concept of Binomial-K-convexity to facilitate the proof that K-convexity is preserved under Binomial propagation in the Bellman function. We then show that the optimal hiring policy exhibits an (s, S)-type structure: when the workforce level in a given period falls below a threshold s, staff are hired up to level S; otherwise, no hiring occurs. Furthermore, we develop a piecewise approximation approach that yields a mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) formulation for solving the problem and computing the (s, S) parameters for each period. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves fast computation with small optimality gaps.

</details>


### [193] [No-gap second-order conditions for optimization problems involving transport distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28264)
*Nicolas Borchard,Christian Meyer,Gerd Wachsmuth*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究测度空间上的优化问题，以到给定先验测度的输运距离作为正则项，利用弱*二阶次微分的理论推导无间隙型二阶最优性条件，证明了弱*上表微分性，并将结果应用于测度空间的最优控制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在测度空间上的优化问题中，引入输运距离作为正则项后，需要建立二阶最优性条件。传统方法难以处理此类非光滑问题的二阶分析，因此需要借助弱*二阶次微分理论，旨在获得无间隙（no-gap）类型的二阶最优性条件。

Method: 采用弱*二阶次微分理论来推导二阶最优性条件；在目标函数的平滑部分和Kantorovich势（对偶输运问题的解）上施加额外假设，证明与二次增长的等价性；计算弱*二阶次微分并证明弱*上表微分性（weak-* epidifferentiability）。

Result: 在额外假设下，建立了一阶与二阶最优性条件与二次增长之间的等价关系（无间隙型）；成功计算了弱*二阶次微分，并证明了弱*上表微分性；所获理论结果成功应用于测度空间中的最优控制问题。

Conclusion: 该工作为以输运距离为正则项的测度空间优化问题提供了完整的二阶变分分析框架，所建立的无间隙二阶最优性条件为数值算法的收敛性分析等后续研究奠定了理论基础，并拓展了最优控制在测度空间中的应用。

Abstract: We consider optimization problems in the space of measures. As a regularization term, the problem includes the transport distance to a given prior measure. For the derivation of second-order optimality conditions of no-gap type, the theory of weak-$\star$ second subderivatives is used which will lead to an equivalence with quadratic growth under additional assumptions on the smooth part of the objective and on the Kantorovich potential, i.e., the solution of the dual transport problem. Further, the weak-$\star$ second subderivative is calculated and weak-$\star$ epidifferentiability is proven. Finally, the results are applied to optimal control problems in measure space.

</details>


### [194] [Exact and Approximate Solvability of Systems Involving Proximity Operators in Hilbert Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28343)
*Mark Allien D. Roble*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究实数Hilbert空间中关于proximity算子方程组的存在性与唯一性，引入了逆近端性质（IPP）作为逆最佳逼近性质（IBAP）的推广，并给出了近似解存在的充要条件及IPP的多重刻画，最后应用于可行性问题和信号恢复问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的投影问题（projection problems）已被广泛研究，但将其推广到更一般的proximity算子系统是一个自然且重要的方向。已有工作中，逆最佳逼近性质（IBAP）在投影系统中扮演重要角色，但对于proximity算子系统，尚缺乏系统性的存在性理论和相应的IPP概念。

Method: 本文在实Hilbert空间中，对有限族proper、下半连续、凸函数的proximity算子方程组建立存在性与唯一性理论。通过引入逆近端性质（IPP）作为IBAP的推广，推导出近似解存在的充要条件，并对IPP给出多种等价刻画。最后将理论应用于可行性问题（feasibility problem）和信号恢复问题（signal recovery problem）以验证其有效性。

Result: 建立了proximity算子方程组精确解和近似解存在的充要条件，提出了IPP概念并给出了若干特征化刻画，证明了该框架在可行性问题和信号恢复问题中的适用性。

Conclusion: 该研究将经典的投影问题推广到更一般的proximity算子框架，所建立的解的存在性与唯一性理论以及IPP性质为优化与逆问题提供了新的工具，在Hilbert空间中具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Let $\HH$ be a real Hilbert space and let $(f_i)_{i\in I}$ be a finite family of proper, lower semicontinuous, and convex functions on $\HH$. This study investigates the existence and uniqueness of exact solutions to systems involving proximity operators of the form: \
  $(\forall i\in I)\ \pr{f_i}(x)=p_i,$
  where $(p_i)_{i\in I}$ is a prescribed collection of proximal points in $\HH$, which naturally generalize classical projection problems. We establish necessary and sufficient conditions for the existence of approximate solutions to such systems. Moreover, we introduce and derive several characterizations of the inverse proximal property (IPP), as a generalization of the inverse best approximation property (IBAP). Applications of the obtained results are presented in the contexts of a feasibility problem and signal recovery problem, demonstrating the relevance of the proposed framework to optimization and inverse problems in Hilbert spaces.

</details>


### [195] [Beyond Hand-Derived Inequalities: Decision Diagrams for Cut Generation in Binary Polynomial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28511)
*Martin Cooper,Margarita Castro*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对二元多项式优化（BPO）的割平面生成问题，提出了一种基于决策图（DD）的通用方法。该方法针对任意选定的支持集（support），在局部多线性多面体中分离出定义面的割，并将其提升回原问题。作者提出了一种基于递归公式的新型紧凑DD编码，仅显式表示顶点变量，而将超边变量隐式编码在状态表示中。此外还获得了多线性多面体的扩展公式、基于有效反链的宽度刻画（揭示了新的多项式可解超图类），以及生成割的面定义性证书。实验表明该方法在根节点实现了更大的间隙闭合，使用更少的割，并显著加速了分支定界过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的强割（如two-links、flowers、odd β-cycles）都是针对固定支持模式手工推导的，缺乏通用性。本文希望提出一种系统化、自动化的割平面生成方法，能够针对任意支持集生成面定义割，从而克服手工推导的局限。

Method: 提出基于决策图（DD）的割平面分离方法。核心创新包括：(1) 一种基于递归公式的紧凑DD编码，只表示顶点变量，超边变量隐式编码在状态中；(2) 从DD编码导出多线性多面体的扩展公式；(3) 利用有效反链刻画宽度，发现新的多项式可解超图类；(4) 提供生成割面定义性的证书；(5) 探索三种支持策略（复用、扩展、分割为section超图）用于割生成。

Result: 实验结果表明，DD-based方法在根节点实现了比现有方法更大的间隙闭合，且使用的割数量更少，同时显著加速了分支定界（branch-and-bound）过程。

Conclusion: 基于决策图的割平面方法为BPO提供了一种通用的、自动化的强割生成框架，克服了传统手工推导特定支持模式割的局限性，在理论和实践上均有显著优势。

Abstract: We study cutting-plane generation for binary polynomial optimization (BPO), whose feasible region is the multilinear set of a hypergraph. Strong inequalities for this set---such as two-links, flowers, and odd $β$-cycles---are classically hand-derived for fixed support patterns. Instead, we propose a decision-diagram (DD) approach: for any chosen support, it separates a facet-defining cut in the local multilinear polytope and lifts it back to the original problem. We utilize a novel compact DD encoding based on a recursive formulation that represents only the vertex variables and implicitly encodes the hyperedge variables inside the state representation. From this DD encoding, we also obtain: (i) an extended formulation of the multilinear polytope, (ii) a width characterization via valid antichains that uncovers a new polynomially solvable class of hypergraphs, and (iii) a certificate for the facetness of the generated cuts. We explore three different support strategies for cut generation that reuse, expand, or partition local structures into section hypergraphs. Our empirical results show that our DD-based methodologies achieve a larger gap closure at the root node with fewer cuts than existing procedures and, in turn, markedly accelerate branch-and-bound procedures.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [Recursive transformers for semiconductor thermo-mechanical reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27251)
*Kart-leong Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了三种递归Transformer范式（Tiny Recursive、Depth Recursive和简单递归Transformer），用于小样本、低维工程代理建模场景。在先进封装热机械可靠性分析和Laplace PDE电容场求解两个任务上验证表明，递归权重共享Transformer能在预测精度、参数效率和计算成本之间取得有效平衡，适合资源受限的工程场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer架构在工程设计中用于替代昂贵的一阶仿真时，往往因小规模、低维数据集而过参数化，导致过拟合和冗余的计算/存储开销。需要转向关注额外计算而非额外可学习参数的架构，以在资源受限条件下实现更优的代理建模。

Method: 提出了硬件感知的三类递归Transformer评估框架：(a) Tiny Recursive Model、(b) 提出的Depth Recursive Transformer、(c) 简单递归Transformer。从预测性能（Recall、MRR）、参数量和计算复杂度（FLOPs）三个维度系统比较，并在两个低维工程预测任务（半导体封装热机可靠性的FEA替代和Laplace PDE电容场迭代求解）上验证。

Result: 递归权重共享Transformer在小数据工程代理建模中提供了精度、参数效率和计算成本的广义权衡。深度递归Transformer在预测性能与资源开销方面表现出有竞争力的平衡，优于简单递归和Tiny递归模型。

Conclusion: 对于小样本、低维的工程代理建模场景，采用递归权重共享的Transformer架构相比常规过参数化Transformer更具优势，可有效缓解过拟合并降低资源开销，为工程设计中递归Transformer架构选择提供了实用指导原则。

Abstract: Transformer-based surrogate models are increasingly used to replace expensive first-principles simulation in engineering design. But conventional transformer architectures are often over parameterized for the small, low-dimensional datasets typical of engineering design spaces, where large simulation data is expensive to generate. Under these conditions, excess parameter capacity leads to overfitting rather than improved accuracy, while also incurring unnecessary memory and compute overhead. This motivates a shift towards architectures that focus on additional compute rather than additional learnable parameters. This paper presents a hardware-aware evaluation of three recursive transformer paradigms for surrogate thermo-mechanical analysis of advanced packages: a)Tiny Recursive Model, b) our proposed Depth Recursive transformer, c) and a simple recursive transformer. We systematically compare their predictive performance (Recall, Mean Reciprocal Rank), parameter count, computational complexity (FLOPs), providing practical design guidelines for selecting recursive transformer architectures under resource-constrained scenarios. We validate this principle on two low-dimensional engineering prediction tasks: 1) thermo-mechanical reliability analysis of advanced semiconductor packages, where stress and warpage from thermal cycling must be evaluated repeatedly across a design-of-experiments sweep under costly finite element analysis (FEA). 2) Laplace PDE iterative numerical solver for capacitance field. Overall, recursive weight-sharing transformers provide an effective and generalizable trade-off between prediction accuracy, parameter efficiency, and computational cost for small data engineering surrogate modeling.

</details>


### [197] [Regularizing modality contribution drift in multimodal continual learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27260)
*Zhen Zhang,Jielei Chu,Bin Liu,Tianrui Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多模态持续学习中的模态贡献漂移（MCD）问题，并设计了一种新的正则化方法CMCDR，通过保持先前任务学习的模态贡献结构来缓解灾难性遗忘，同时支持有回放和无回放两种设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态持续学习方法通常关注跨模态表示对齐或语义相似性，但忽略了各模态的独立贡献及其交互作用在增量任务中的稳定性。这种决策层面的漂移（被称为模态贡献漂移MCD）是导致遗忘的关键因素，且现有方法无法可靠地缓解这一问题。

Method: 作者首先定义了MCD分数，通过受控的模态子集干预来量化贡献强度与相对依赖性的变化。然后提出CMCDR方法，包含两个版本：(1) 基于回放的版本，将模态子集干预作为诊断探针作用于存储的旧样本，对比当前模型与冻结的旧模型之间的贡献轮廓，并约束旧样本中模态特定和交互贡献的变化；(2) 无回放版本，使用当前任务样本作为探针，蒸馏冻结模型中旧任务的贡献响应，从而无需示例即可正则化观察到的贡献轮廓。

Result: 在模态类增量学习和持续视觉问答任务上的实验验证了CMCDR的通用性和有效性，表明该方法能够缓解模态贡献漂移并减少遗忘。

Conclusion: 通过显式地保持先前学习任务的模态贡献结构，CMCDR为多模态持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题提供了一种新的解决思路，并且其回放和无回放两个版本使其能够适应不同的实际场景。

Abstract: Multimodal continual learning (MMCL) aims to learn emerging knowledge from multimodal data while preserving knowledge. To mitigate forgetting, current MMCL methods usually focus on cross-modal representation alignment or semantic similarity, but they overlook whether the relative contributions of individual modalities and their interactions remain stable across incremental tasks. We term this decision-level shift Modality Contribution Drift (MCD) and quantify it with the MCD score, which combines contribution-strength and relative-reliance changes under controlled interventions on modality subsets. Theoretical and empirical analyses further explain why current MMCL methods cannot reliably mitigate this drift. To this end, we propose Continual Modality Contribution Drift Regularization (CMCDR), which preserves the modality contribution structure of previously learned tasks. Since MMCL settings differ in whether old exemplars are available, CMCDR includes both replay-based and replay-free versions. The replay-based version uses modality-subset interventions as diagnostic probes on stored old samples, compares their contribution profiles between the current model and a frozen previous model, and constrains changes in old-sample modality-specific and interaction contributions. The replay-free version uses current-task samples as probes and distills the frozen model's old-task contribution responses, thereby regularizing the observed contribution profile without exemplars. Experiments on multimodal class-incremental learning and continual visual question answering validate the generality and effectiveness of CMCDR.

</details>


### [198] [DoTime: A Synthetic Benchmark Generator for Interventional and Counterfactual Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27263)
*Dennis Thumm,Billy Tim Anthony,Ying Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍DoTime，一个开放、可扩展、理论上严谨的多变量时序结构因果模型（TSCM）生成器，支持干预和反事实数据生成，并发布dotime PyPI包及四个冻结评估套件。它引入连续时间干预窗口、带积极性保护的反事实采样、体制切换SCM、非平稳动态等新能力，并通过实验验证了干预训练相比同等容量观测模型在方向精度上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列因果推理基准大多是观测性、小规模或领域特定的，缺乏干预和反事实估计的评测，尤其在医疗、政策评估和气候科学等关键领域。需要一种开放、可扩展、理论上扎实的数据生成器来填补这一空白。

Method: 提出DoTime：一个多变量时序结构因果模型（TSCM）生成器，以dotime PyPI包发布，并附带四个冻结评估套件。相比既有生成器，它增加了几项关键能力：连续时间干预窗口、带积极性保护的反事实采样模式、作为中断时间序列严格泛化的体制切换SCM、通过切换SCM参数构造的非平稳动态、将趋势和结构断点置于评估窗口内的确定性斜坡和正弦干预轮廓。并实现了一个因果基础模型的参考实现作为生成器先验。套件包含10万条轨迹的训练规模快照和8个命名识别结构，每种结构都有精确真值：全程来自同一SCM的配对干预轨迹，以及连续时间套件中的共享噪声反事实。同时提供参考基线实现和评估框架，并提出可证伪声明：干预训练相比同等容量观测模型在方向精度上带来可测优势。

Result: 在结构匹配的留出片段评估中，干预先验拟合网络（PFN）的精度差距在每种结构、轨迹长度和训练种子下均为正值。该声明在三个训练种子的每个臂上进行了测试，实验结果支持干预训练确实带来方向精度优势这一假设。

Conclusion: DoTime提供了一个理论上严谨、可扩展且开放的时间序列因果生成器，填补了干预和反事实估计基准的空白。它不仅引入了多项前所未有的生成能力，还验证了干预训练对因果基础模型的提升效果，为时间序列因果推理研究提供了新的基准资源。

Abstract: Most benchmarks for causal inference over time series are observational, small, or domain-specific, leaving interventional and counterfactual estimation under-served exactly where it matters most, such as in healthcare, policy evaluation, and climate science. We introduce \textbf{DoTime}, an open, scalable, and theoretically grounded generator of multivariate temporal structural causal models (TSCMs) with interventions, released as the \code{dotime} PyPI package together with four frozen evaluation suites. Beyond existing work, it adds capabilities absent from prior generators: continuous-time intervention \emph{windows}, counterfactual sampling modes with a positivity guard, regime-switching SCMs as a strict generalization of interrupted time series, non-stationary dynamics by construction with switching SCM parameters, and deterministic ramp and sinusoidal intervention profiles that place trends and structural breaks \emph{inside} the evaluation window. Moreover, it demonstrates the suitability of the generator as a prior for a causal foundation model reference implementation. The released suites span a training-scale snapshot of $100{,}000$ trajectories and eight named identification structures, each with exact ground truth: paired interventional trajectories from the same SCM throughout, and shared-noise counterfactuals in the continuous-time suite. We ship reference baseline implementations with an evaluation harness, and pose a falsifiable claim: interventional training buys a measurable direction-accuracy advantage over an observational model of identical capacity. It is tested across three training seeds per arm. Under structure-matched evaluation on held-out episodes, the interventional prior-fitted network's (PFN) gap is positive in every structure, trajectory length, and seed tested.

</details>


### [199] [PlatformBid: An Auto-Bidding Benchmark from a Unified Advertising Platform's Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27265)
*Shengtian Yang,Yewen Li,Peng Jiang,Zhiyi Lyu,Bo An,Peng Jiang,Qingpeng Cai,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PlatformBid——首个从统一广告平台（集成SSP、DSP和Ad Exchange）视角出发的自动竞价基准，涵盖三种代表性场景，并在此基础上提出基于flow-matching的新型竞价方法BidFlow。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动竞价研究仅关注DSP侧，以最大化广告主转化率为目标，而现实中大型广告平台已内部整合SSP、DSP和Ad Exchange，其目标不仅是广告主转化，还包括平台总收益。目前缺乏平台视角的系统性评估框架，亟需统一的评测基准来推动自动竞价研究发展。

Method: 1）构建PlatformBid基准，从统一广告平台视角设计三个代表性设置：同质竞争（广告主采用相同算法）、异质竞争（多样算法策略共存）和促销竞争（广告主在促销日爆发性增加预算）；2）系统评估现有自动竞价方法（经典控制方法、基于强化学习的方法和近期生成式方法）；3）提出BidFlow，基于flow-matching的生成式竞价方法，利用其表达能力强的策略表征来应对动态竞争环境。

Result: 在快手平台上进行在线实验，BidFlow在目标成本上实现了+0.68%的改进，验证了PlatformBid基准离线与在线结果的一致性。

Conclusion: PlatformBid作为首个平台视角的自动竞价基准，能够有效支撑自动竞价算法的系统评估，且BidFlow方法在真实平台部署中表现出性能优势，为离线-在线一致性提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Real-time bidding is central to computational advertising, comprising three elements: Supply Side Platform (SSP) selling ad impressions, Demand Side Platform (DSP) bidding for advertisers, and Ad Exchange conducting auctions between them. Traditional auto-bidding algorithms focus solely on the DSP side, maximizing advertiser conversions by adjusting bids against competitors. However, current big ad platforms, such as social media and e-commerce companies, now integrate SSP, DSP, and Ad Exchange functions internally. From such ad platforms' perspective, the goal of the auto-bidding algorithms is not only to maximize the advertisers' conversions, but also the total revenue of the platform. Given the lack of platform-centric evaluation frameworks and the pressing need to advance auto-bidding research, we propose PlatformBid - the first comprehensive benchmark designed from a unified ad platform's perspective. To accurately reflect the real-world auto-bidding scenarios, we define three representative settings: (1) homogeneous competition with identical algorithms across advertisers, (2) heterogeneous competition with diverse algorithmic strategies, and (3) promotional competition where some advertisers surge budgets for boosting sales during promotional events like Black Friday. We systematically evaluate a broad spectrum of existing auto-bidding methods across these settings, encompassing classical control methods, RL-based methods, and recent generative methods. Besides these methods, we further propose a novel auto-bidding method based on flow-matching, termed BidFlow, which leverages the flow-matching method's expressive policy representation to effectively handle dynamic competitive environments. Online experiments on Kuaishou further show a +0.68\% improvement in target cost, providing deployment evidence for the offline-online consistency of PlatformBid.

</details>


### [200] [Beyond KV Reconstruction: Functional Reconstruction for MLA Draft Models in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27269)
*Weiye Shi,Fanxu Meng,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端(E2E)的功能重构方法，用于将MHA/GQA模型转换为MLA架构。该方法通过优化每个转换后的MLA注意力模块，使其在校准隐藏状态上复现原始MHA/GQA模型的输出投影结果，从而克服直接转换导致的投机解码接受率下降问题。在192种配置（涵盖4个Llama/Qwen模型对、TransMLA和MHA2MLA转换器、HF和vLLM后端、4个200提示词任务）中，该方法在64个匹配任务单元中的37个显著提升了接受率，26个基本不变，仅1个下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLA因其紧凑的潜在状态替代增长的KV缓存、减少长上下文推理的解码内存流量而日益重要，但大多数开放权重的检查点使用MHA/GQA，需要转换才能获得MLA的缓存效率而无需从头训练。然而，直接进行MHA/GQA到MLA的转换会因低秩分解和RoPE处理引入注意力函数误差，虽然对独立生成可能可容忍，但会大幅降低投机解码中草稿令牌的接受率。因此需要一种能够保留转换后缓存和推理图、且无需验证器监督的转换后优化方法。

Method: 作者将MLA草稿构建形式化为功能性重构而非缓存压缩。具体地，E2E方法在转换后优化每个MLA注意力模块，使其在校准隐藏状态上复现原始MHA/GQA对应模块的输出投影（post-output-projection）响应。该方法是转换器无关的转换后处理程序，保留了转换后的缓存和推理图结构，不依赖验证器logits或验证器监督。

Result: 在192个模型-转换器-后端-方法-任务配置上评估，涵盖4个Llama/Qwen草稿-目标模型对、TransMLA和MHA2MLA、HF和vLLM后端以及4个200提示词任务。以0.5个百分点为报告容差，功能重构在64个匹配任务单元中显著提升了37个的接受率，26个基本不变，仅1个显著下降。

Conclusion: 提出的功能性重构方法能有效解决直接MHA/GQA到MLA转换导致的投机解码草稿接受率下降问题，且不改变转换后的缓存和推理图，无需验证器监督，适用于多种转换器、后端和模型组合。代码和评估工件已开源。

Abstract: Multi-head latent attention (MLA) is increasingly important for long-context LLM inference because compact latent states replace the growing key-value (KV) cache and reduce decoding memory traffic. Yet most capable open checkpoints use multi-head or grouped-query attention (MHA/GQA), so conversion is needed to obtain MLA's cache efficiency without retraining from scratch. Speculative decoding offers complementary acceleration, but its speedup depends on agreement between draft proposals and target verification. We find that direct MHA/GQA-to-MLA conversion can sharply reduce this agreement: low-rank factorization and RoPE handling introduce attention-function errors that may be tolerable for standalone generation but substantially lower draft-token acceptance. We therefore formulate MLA draft construction as functional reconstruction rather than cache compression. Our end-to-end (E2E) method optimizes each converted MLA attention module to reproduce the post-output-projection response of its original MHA/GQA counterpart on calibration hidden states. This converter-agnostic post-conversion procedure preserves the converted cache and inference graph and requires neither verifier logits nor verifier supervision. We evaluate 192 model-converter-backend-method-task configurations spanning four Llama/Qwen draft-target pairs, TransMLA and MHA2MLA, HF and vLLM, and four 200-prompt tasks. With a 0.5-percentage-point reporting tolerance, Functional Reconstruction materially improves acceptance in 37 of 64 matched task cells, leaves 26 practically unchanged, and materially decreases one. Code and evaluation artifacts are available at https://github.com/swyhahaha/FunctionalMLA.

</details>


### [201] [RLPF: Reinforcement Learning from Performance Feedback for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27271)
*Huihao Jing,Haozhe Cui,Wenbin Hu,Shaojin Chen,Haochen Shi,Changxuan Fan,Yuxuan Liu,Hanyu Yang,Sirui Zhang,Ziyi Chen,Haoran Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出RLPF（基于性能反馈的强化学习），将执行结果转化为分阶段奖励：失败的程序按执行进度排序，正确的程序按其相对基线到专家参考的改进排名，从而训练代码智能体不仅通过测试，还能优化程序运行效率。在PerfCodeBench上，Qwen3-32B的正确可运行解决方案从11.1%提升到54.6%，相对效率从8.1%提升到38.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码模型越来越多地使用执行反馈进行训练，但大多数训练信号停留在正确性上。对于系统代码，两个程序可能通过相同测试，但运行时间差异巨大。运行时间是一个脆弱的奖励信号：仅在程序正确后才有意义，跨任务变化大，且当大多数采样程序无法编译或运行时提供很少的指导。因此需要一种方法来训练代码智能体优先选择更快的正确实现，而不仅仅将效率作为评估指标。

Method: 提出RLPF（Reinforcement Learning from Performance Feedback），将执行结果转化为分阶段奖励：1）对于失败的程序，按执行进度排序（编译/运行的不同阶段）；2）对于正确的程序，按其相对基线到专家参考的效率改进进行排名。这提供了正确性之前的有效反馈和正确性之后的性能敏感反馈。

Result: 在PerfCodeBench上，使用RLPF微调Qwen3-32B后，正确且可运行的解决方案从11.1%提升到54.6%，相对效率从8.1%提升到38.6%。训练后的模型与更强的开源权重系统竞争，优化行为可适度迁移到EffiBench-X。额外研究表明模型生成参考提供有用但较弱的监督，完整组合奖励比仅正确性或仅运行时基线更可靠。

Conclusion: 代码智能体可以被训练不仅通过测试，还能优化其编写的程序。通过将执行结果转化为分阶段奖励信号，可以在正确性之前和之后都提供有用的反馈，从而同时提升代码的正确率和运行效率。

Abstract: Code models are increasingly trained with execution feedback, but most training signals still stop at correctness. This leaves an important gap for systems code: two programs can pass the same tests while differing greatly in runtime. We study how to train code agents to prefer faster correct implementations, rather than treating efficiency only as an evaluation metric. The key difficulty is that runtime is a fragile reward. It is meaningful only after a program is correct, varies across tasks, and gives little guidance when most sampled programs fail to compile or run. We propose \textbf{RLPF}, reinforcement learning from performance feedback, which turns execution outcomes into a staged reward. Failed programs are ordered by execution progress, while correct programs are ranked by their relative improvement from the baseline toward the expert reference. This gives useful feedback before correctness and performance-sensitive feedback after correctness. Fine-tuning Qwen3-32B with RLPF on PerfCodeBench raises correct-and-runnable solutions from $11.1\%$ to $54.6\%$ and improves relative efficiency from $8.1\%$ to $38.6\%$. The trained model becomes competitive with stronger open-weight systems, and its optimization behavior transfers modestly to EffiBench-X. Additional studies show that model-generated references provide useful but weaker supervision, and that the full composite reward is more reliable than correctness-only or runtime-only baselines. These results suggest that code agents can be trained not only to pass tests, but also to optimize the programs they write.

</details>


### [202] [SDO: Structure-Aware Data Organization for Efficient LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27273)
*Jinliang Gao,Ning Yang,Hai Wang,Baili Xiao,Pin Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SDO，一个即插即用的数据组织框架，通过暴露驱动的反馈机制按表示空间结构组织小批量组成和样本暴露频率，在SFT、DPO和GRPO上加速收敛并提升跨题目类型的准确率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型后训练效率优化主要聚焦于样本选择或训练调度，但数据组织本身被视为静态预处理步骤，固定的嵌入分组在训练中无法适应样本暴露的演变，导致部分样本冗余更新而其他样本欠优化。

Method: SDO框架包含两个核心机制：1) 批次内局部感知批处理，通过KNN邻域遍历形成连贯的小批量；2) 跨epoch的暴露均衡调度，记录每个样本的参与次数并降低过暴露样本的采样概率以保持长期覆盖。运行在冻结的外部嵌入上，逐epoch操作，避免模型预热训练开销。

Result: 在SFT、DPO和GRPO三种后训练场景中，SDO均加速了收敛，在训练早期到中期阶段收益最大，产生更连贯的梯度，并在不永久排除训练样本的前提下实现跨问题类型更均衡的准确率。

Conclusion: 动态的数据组织能替代静态预处理步骤，根据样本暴露情况自适应调整，以更高效的样本利用实现更好的训练效果。

Abstract: Post-training of large language models is expensive, and existing efficiency improvements mainly focus on selecting informative samples or designing training schedules. However, data organization itself is usually treated as a static preprocessing step: embedding-based grouping methods construct fixed partitions before training and cannot adapt to the evolving sample exposure during optimization. As a result, all samples receive similar exposure despite their different optimization needs, leading to redundant updates for some samples while leaving others under-optimized. To address this problem, we propose SDO (Structure-Aware Data Organization), a plug-and-play data organization framework with an exposure-driven feedback mechanism that organizes mini-batch composition and sample exposure according to representation-space structure. SDO operates epoch by epoch on frozen external embeddings, avoiding model warm-up training overhead: within each epoch, locality-aware batching forms coherent mini-batches via KNN neighborhood traversal; across epochs, exposure-balanced scheduling records per-sample participation and reduces the sampling probability of over-exposed samples to preserve long-term coverage. Across SFT, DPO, and GRPO, SDO accelerates convergence, with the largest gains observed in the early-to-mid phase, producing more coherent gradients and more balanced accuracy across question types without permanently excluding training samples.

</details>


### [203] [Rethinking EEG-Based Disease Diagnosis: Decoupling Instance Representation Learning from Subject-Level Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27274)
*Zhiyuan Ma,Zeyuan Li,Zhiyi Lu,Jiacheng Hao,Youlang Du,Zhen Jiang,Xinche Zhang,Yuhao Sun,Sen Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BridgeMIL，一个两阶段框架用于基于EEG的疾病诊断。第一阶段通过对比学习和正则化预训练实例编码器（不分配实例级标签），第二阶段将编码器迁移到基于注意力机制的MIL聚合器，仅在受试者级别施加监督。在三个EEG疾病数据集和五个骨干网络上，BridgeMIL在15个设置中的14个达到最高平均准确率，整体平均准确率76.57%，比最强基线高4.28个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG疾病诊断本质上需要每个受试者一个预测，但现有的流水线将记录切分为短实例并把受试者标签继承给每个实例，训练实例级分类器，这假设所有实例具有同等的诊断证据可靠性。MIL可以避免继承标签，但EEG数据集的受试者数量远少于实例数量，限制了端到端MIL学习的表示质量。

Method: 两阶段框架。阶段1：不使用实例级继承标签，通过时间近邻窗口对齐和独立采样的受试者内子包对齐来预训练编码器；使用方差和协方差正则化防止表示坍缩并降低冗余，无需负样本对。阶段2：将编码器迁移到注意力机制MIL聚合器，仅对受试者预测施加监督，并通过特征保留（feature retention）限制表示漂移。

Result: 在三个EEG疾病数据集和五个代表性骨干网络上，BridgeMIL在15个数据集-骨干设置中的14个取得最高平均准确率，整体平均准确率76.57%，比最强基线高4.28个百分点。分析发现实例继承标签可靠性存在显著差异，模型对受试者稀缺比实例稀缺更敏感，且学到的表示空间更结构化，具有清晰的受试者级聚类和改进的诊断类别分离。

Conclusion: 监督应与受试者级别的预测目标对齐，同时利用大量EEG实例学习而不为单个实例分配疾病标签，这种解耦策略能显著提升EEG疾病诊断性能。

Abstract: EEG-based disease diagnosis requires one prediction per subject, yet common pipelines segment recordings into short instances, inherit the subject label for every instance, and train instance-level classifiers. This assumes that all instances provide equally reliable diagnostic evidence. Multiple instance learning (MIL) avoids inherited labels by treating each subject as a bag. However, EEG datasets contain far fewer subjects than instances, which can limit the quality of the representations learned by end-to-end MIL. We propose BridgeMIL, a two-stage framework that decouples instance representation learning from subject-level supervision. Stage 1 pretrains the encoder without inherited instance labels by aligning temporally nearby windows and independently sampled within-subject sub-bags. Variance and covariance regularization prevent collapse and reduce redundancy without negative pairs. Stage 2 transfers the encoder to an attention-based MIL aggregator, applies supervision only to subject predictions, and limits representation drift through feature retention. Across three EEG disease datasets and five representative backbones, BridgeMIL attains the highest mean accuracy in 14 of 15 dataset-backbone settings and an overall mean accuracy of 76.57%, 4.28 percentage points higher than the strongest baseline. Further analyses reveal substantial variation in inherited-label reliability across instances, greater performance sensitivity to subject scarcity than to instance scarcity, and a more structured representation space with distinct subject-wise clusters and improved separation between diagnostic classes. Together, these findings underscore the importance of aligning supervision with the subject-level prediction objective while learning from abundant EEG instances without assigning disease labels to individual instances.

</details>


### [204] [Strategies for Milestone-driven Start-ups in Multi-activity Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27563)
*Zhengli Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究初创企业在里程碑导向环境下的最优策略选择问题。通过构建连续时间随机控制模型，其中企业状态由扩散过程刻画，创业者可在多种活动（控制）间选择，这些活动具有不同的成本和漂移/方差特性。企业达到上边界即为成功，触及下边界则为失败。作者通过"高效前沿曲线"对控制进行排序（基于风险性：漂移-波动率比，以及成本效益：漂移-成本比），并完全求解了最优策略的显式结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 初创企业需要在多个阶段达成里程碑目标才能"生存"，但现有研究缺乏在里程碑导向情境下如何选择多种活动策略的系统性分析。特别是当控制数量不少于三个时，连续时间随机控制模型通常极其困难。

Method: 构建连续时间随机控制模型，将初创企业状态建模为扩散过程。创业者可在多种控制间选择（每种控制具有不同的成本和漂移/方差参数），企业达到上边界（成功）或下边界（失败）时终局。作者通过定义高效前沿曲线（基于漂移-波动率比和漂移-成本比两个直观指标）来排序控制，并完全求解了最优策略的结构。

Result: 完全求解了最优策略并给出其结构的显式刻画。最优策略仅使用由高效前沿曲线表征的控制集合。一个独特发现是：根据模型参数的不同，高效前沿曲线可以呈现不同类型的形状，导致最优策略产生定性的结构差异。这是首个能容纳多种类型高效前沿曲线的随机控制模型。

Conclusion: 该工作为初创企业提供了评估活动的直观指标（风险性和成本效益），并揭示了里程碑导向情境下最优策略如何根据具体情景发生定性变化。研究结果为创业决策研究奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: New venture start-ups need to ``survive'' through multiple stages of reaching milestone targets. We investigate the strategies for start-ups in a milestone-oriented setting. We examine a model of an entrepreneurial start-up firm, where its state is captured by a diffusion process. The entrepreneur can choose between multiple activities (or controls), which incur different cost and determine the drift and the variance of the process. Depending on whether the process reaches a fixed upper boundary or a lower one, the start-up firm succeeds or fails. Continuous-time stochastic models with multiple ($\ge 3$) controls are typically very challenging to deal with. In this work, we are able to completely solve for the optimal policy and provide an explicit characterization of its structure. In particular, the optimal policy only uses controls from a set characterized by a so-called efficient frontier curve that orders the controls by two intuitive measures: riskiness (drift-to-volatility ratio) and cost-effectiveness (drift-to-cost ratio). A unique feature of our model is that depending on the model parameters, the efficient frontier curves can be of different types, resulting in qualitatively different structures of the optimal policy. As far as we know, this is the first study that analyzes a stochastic control model which admits efficient frontier curves of different types. Our work provides start-up firms with intuitive measures to evaluate their activities and offers valuable insights on how the optimal strategies in a milestone-oriented setting change qualitatively contingent upon the specific scenario. We believe the results provide a foundational block in the study of entrepreneurial decision-making.

</details>


### [205] [Flat Score, Amplified Failures: How the Error Budget Masks Damage in Quantized LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27275)
*Jiwon Jang,Kisu Yang,Heuiseok Lim,Hyunwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 4比特权重量化在标准指标上看似无损，但在多轮工具调用智能体任务中会放大模型已有的失败模式（如工具名幻觉），错误量最高增加2.5倍，而基准测试的十个错误预算掩盖了这一损伤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4比特权重量化被广泛报道为近乎无损，但这一结论是否适用于当前最重要的场景——多轮工具调用智能体——尚未得到验证。作者旨在检验这一说法并揭示标准指标可能掩盖的退化。

Method: 在τ²-bench上，对两个开源模型系列（含稠密和MoE变体）在两个领域共八个单元进行测试，每个单元456个回合，分别在16位、8位和4位权重下评估。采用标准指标、多元比较校正、等价性检验、错误过程分析（按错误通道）、秩相关分析、收缩预算诊断和针对性错误修复提示等方法。

Result: 标准分数无显著变化（无单元通过多重比较校正，等价性检验将变化限制在±7.5分内），但量化将已有失败（电信领域的工具名幻觉，零售领域同方向实体错误趋势）量放大至最多2.5倍（每任务+17.6分），几乎不产生新失败。失败集合在所有精度下高度一致（秩相关≥0.94，仅0.18%新事件）。将错误预算从十个收缩到两个时，仅在量化增加错误量的单元重新暴露17分的分数差距。

Conclusion: 标准任务奖励指标掩盖了量化放大已有错误的效应——基准测试的宽松错误预算吸收了额外失败。作者建议在报告任务奖励的同时，报告每个通道的错误率和收缩预算下的成功率，因为这两个诊断均来自基准测试已收集的日志数据。

Abstract: Post-training quantization to 4-bit weights is widely reported to be nearly lossless. We test this claim for multi-turn, tool-calling agents, where it now matters most. On $τ^2$-bench, across two open-weight model families in dense and MoE variants and two domains (eight cells, 456 episodes each, at 16-, 8-, and 4-bit weights), quantization indeed looks free on the standard metric. No cell shows a score change that survives multiple-comparison correction, and in the cell that carries the largest process damage, equivalence testing bounds the change within $\pm$7.5 points. The process tells a different story. Quantization amplifies the failure the model already exhibits at full precision (tool-name hallucination in telecom, with the same directional trend in retail entity errors) by up to 2.5$\times$ in volume (+17.6 points per task), while creating essentially no new failures.
